```
# S2-011 / S2-012 — Answers from Review Evidence

**Stories:** [#62](https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/issues/62) S2-011,
[#63](https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/issues/63) S2-012 |
**Rules:** S2-BR-008 through S2-BR-015 | **Builds on:** S2-013 (PR #76), S2-010 (PR #78)

## Pipeline

`answerQuestion(targetId, question, { buildReviewDatasetContext, callModel })`
(`src/services/qa/answer-question.ts`):

1. **Context (S2-010):** `buildReviewDatasetContext(targetId)` loads the signed-in owner's reviews
   for the active target, or throws.
2. **Request (S2-013):** `buildQaModelRequest` builds the system instructions, the review dataset,
   and the question, and checks the request-size budget, or throws `QaPromptError`.
3. **Model call (S2-011):** `callModel(request)`. In production this is `createOpenAiQaModel()`;
   tests pass a deterministic fake.
4. **Reply validation (S2-011 / S2-012):** `parseQaModelReply` checks the reply, or throws
   `QaResponseError`.

A failure at any step throws, and nothing unvalidated is returned. Steps 1 and 2 run before any
model call.

| Error                                       | From          | Meaning                                            |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
| `ReviewContextError`, `TargetNotFoundError` | S2-010        | Not signed in, foreign or missing target, no data  |
| `QaPromptError`                             | S2-013        | Empty question, mismatched data, request too large |
| `QaModelError`                              | OpenAI client | Disabled, not configured, provider failure         |
| `QaResponseError`                           | Validation    | Malformed or ungrounded reply                      |

Every error message is user-safe. None includes review text, model output, provider bodies, or
the API key.

## Reply validation (`qa-response.ts`)

The model must reply with exactly `{"outcome", "answer", "citedReviewIds"}`. Bad replies are
rejected, not repaired:

- `INVALID_MODEL_RESPONSE`: not JSON, an unknown outcome, missing or extra fields, an empty answer,
  an answer over `MAX_ANSWER_CHARACTERS` (8,000), or non-string citations.
- `UNGROUNDED_ANSWER`:
  - a cited review ID that was not in the request (invented, or from another target); or
  - `outcome: "answered"` with no citations. S2-011 requires answers that are defensibly
    connected to the reviews, so an uncited "answer" is treated as a general-knowledge guess.

Accepted replies are normalized: the answer is trimmed, and duplicate citations are removed.
Decline outcomes never carry citations. `insufficient_evidence` may cite reviews for the part of
the question they do answer (prompt evidence rule 4).

## S2-012: insufficient evidence
### Configuration

| Variable          | Required              | Notes                                                   |
| ----------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| `QA_ENABLED`      | Yes, exactly `"true"` | Any other value disables Q&A before any network request |
| `OPENAI_API_KEY`  | When enabled          | Server-only. Never `NEXT_PUBLIC_`.                      |
| `OPENAI_QA_MODEL` | When enabled          | Team decision. There is deliberately no default.        |

Configuration is read on every call, so a missing key or a disabled flag fails closed.

### API verification

Checked against the official OpenAI API reference and structured-outputs guide on 2026-11-12.

- **Confirmed:** endpoint, method, and auth header; `instructions` as a system/developer message;
  `input` as a string; `max_output_tokens` (minimum 16); `store`; the
  `text.format` json_schema nesting; the message content shapes `output_text` / `refusal`.
- **Not confirmed from the page:** the full list of top-level `status` values, the `usage` field
  names, and whether `output_text` appears in the raw REST body. The client is written so that
  each of these fails closed: only `completed` is accepted, usage is optional, and text is read
  from `output[]`. Confirm them with one manual call before enabling Q&A.

## Not in this change

- **Q&A Route Handler and UI (S2-009):** the route must use `requireApiUser()`, apply the
  question length limit, pass S2-010's `buildReviewDatasetContext` and `createOpenAiQaModel()`
  into `answerQuestion`, and map each error class to a response.
- **S2-010 wiring:** `buildReviewDatasetContext` is injected because PR #78 is not merged yet.
- **Live evaluation:** the S2-003 harness and the S2-004 evaluation set measure real model
  behavior. Q&A stays disabled (`QA_ENABLED`) until that run and team review.
- **Model choice:** `OPENAI_QA_MODEL` is a team decision.
```

Any failure throws, and the context and request are built before any model call. The context builder and the model caller are injected.\n- `src/services/qa/qa-response.ts`: `parseQaModelReply()` and the strict JSON schema sent to the model.\n- `src/services/qa/openai-qa-client.ts`: OpenAI Responses API adapter (`createOpenAiQaModel()`).\n- `src/services/qa/system-prompt.ts`: prompt `s2-013.v3` citation contract (see below).\n- 3 new test files plus additions to the S2-013 tests: 100% coverage of all QA modules.\n- `docs/s2-011-answers.md`, `src/services/README.md`, `.env.example` (`QA_ENABLED=false`, `OPENAI_API_KEY`, `OPENAI_QA_MODEL`).\n\nRefs #62, #63\n\n## Integration entry point\n\nThe Q&A route (S2-009) calls **`answerQuestion(targetId, question, { buildReviewDatasetContext, callModel })`**. It is built on **`runGroundedQaRequest()`** (S2-013, `qa-request.ts`), which builds the S2-010 context and the model request and stops before the model call if either throws. `answerQuestion()` adds the reply validation on top. A route should not call `runGroundedQaRequest()` directly, because that would skip reply validation.\n\nProduction wiring (after #78 merges):\n\n```ts\nanswerQuestion(targetId, question, {\n  buildReviewDatasetContext, // S2-010\n  callModel: createOpenAiQaModel(), // reads QA_ENABLED / OPENAI_API_KEY / OPENAI_QA_MODEL\n});\n```\n\n## Running it with synthetic data (test environment)\n\nNo database, network, or API key is needed:\n\n```ts\nconst context = {\n  target: { id: \"t1\", name: \"Synthetic Cafe\", platform: \"google_maps\" as const },\n  reviews: [{ id: \"r1\", analysisTargetId: \"t1\", rating: 2, text: \"Slow service.\", reviewDate: null }],\n};\n\nawait answerQuestion(\"t1\", \"Any complaints?\", {\n  buildReviewDatasetContext: async () => context,\n  // A fake model...\n  callModel: async () => ({\n    text: JSON.stringify({ outcome: \"answered\", answer: \"Slow service.\", citedReviewIds: [\"r1\"] }),\n  }),\n  // ...or the real adapter with a fake fetch, to inspect the outgoing request:\n  // callModel: createOpenAiQaModel({ fetcher: fakeFetch, env: { QA_ENABLED: \"true\", OPENAI_API_KEY: \"test\", OPENAI_QA_MODEL: \"test-model\" } }),\n});\n```\n\nRun the suites with `npx jest tests/unit/services/qa`. The controlled live smoke test (real key, synthetic data) is a separate manual step after the team approves the integration contract and spending limit.\n\n## S2-011: reply validation\n\nReplies are rejected, not repaired:\n\n| Outcome | No citations | Valid citations | Unknown citation IDs |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| `answered` | `UNGROUNDED_ANSWER` | Accepted | `UNGROUNDED_ANSWER` |\n| `insufficient_evidence` | Accepted | `UNEXPECTED_CITATIONS` | `UNGROUNDED_ANSWER` |\n| `declined_*` (all three) | Accepted | `UNEXPECTED_CITATIONS` | `UNGROUNDED_ANSWER` |\n\nMalformed replies (not JSON, unknown outcome, missing or extra fields, empty or over-8,000-character answer) are `INVALID_MODEL_RESPONSE`. Citations on declines and `insufficient_evidence` are **rejected, not silently dropped** (review follow-up). The prompt (`s2-013.v3`) states the same contract: a partial answer uses `answered` with citations and says what the reviews do not cover; every other outcome must have an empty `citedReviewIds`.\n\n## S2-012: insufficient evidence\n\nThe tests use a controlled dataset (food and service reviews only) and the issue's question, \"Do customers think the parking lot is safe at night?\":\n- the dataset sent to the model contains no parking evidence;\n- an `insufficient_evidence` reply with no citations passes through with its limitation stated;\n- an `answered` reply with no supporting reviews is rejected.\n\n## Known limitations\n\n- **Citation support is not verified.** The validator confirms each cited review ID belongs to the active dataset, but not that the review supports the claim. For example, a parking-safety claim citing a food review passes (a test documents this). Semantic support is covered by the **S2-004 evaluations**, which should include unsupported-citation cases.\n- **Fake-model tests.** These tests check the pipeline, not live model judgment. Live behavior needs the S2-003 harness and the S2-004 evaluation set.\n- **Token estimate.** Request size is estimated from UTF-8 bytes (3 per token). The smoke test should compare it with the provider-reported `usage`.\n\n## Request budget and provider gating\n\n- **Budget:** the 16,000-token application cap (`QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS`) and 1,500-token output reservation (`QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS`) from #76 are unchanged. The full request (prompt + evidence + question + reserved output) is checked **before** the provider call. An end-to-end test with the real OpenAI adapter shows an oversized request never reaches the provider.\n- **Disabled by default:** `QA_ENABLED=false` in `.env.example`. Tests confirm the provider (injected and global `fetch`) is never called when `QA_ENABLED` is `false` or unset, and that `.env.example` ships disabled.\n- **Model:** read only from `OPENAI_QA_MODEL`, with **no fallback**. If it is unset, the call fails with `MODEL_NOT_CONFIGURED` before any network request; `QaModelError.detail` says \"OPENAI_QA_MODEL is not set. There is no default model; set it explicitly.\" for server logs. A missing key is `API_KEY_NOT_CONFIGURED`.\n\n## Security notes (for the §7 security review: third-party call and AI data boundary)\n\n- **Off by default:** no network request unless `QA_ENABLED` is exactly `\"true\"` and both `OPENAI_API_KEY` and `OPENAI_QA_MODEL` are set. Configuration is read on every call.\n- **Provider-input isolation:** the system prompt is sent only as `instructions`; review text and the question only as `input` (user message). Tested.\n- **No retention requested:** `store: false`, because the request contains customer review text.\n- **Bounded:** `max_output_tokens` from the budget, a 30 s timeout (also covers reading the body), and a 256 KiB response cap.\n- **Fail closed:** only `status: \"completed\"` is accepted; `incomplete`, `refusal`, malformed bodies, and HTTP errors map to fixed codes. No user-facing error message contains provider bodies, model output, review text, or the API key (tested with marker values).\n- **Server only:** all modules import `server-only`. No new dependencies; the API is called with `fetch`.\n\n## API verification\n\nChecked against the official OpenAI API reference and structured-outputs guide. Confirmed: endpoint, method, auth header, `instructions`, string `input`, `max_output_tokens`, `store`, `text.format` json_schema nesting, and the `output_text` / `refusal` content shapes. Per Shawn's review, the response text and `usage` fields are also documented in the [official reference](https://developers.openai.com/api/reference/resources/responses/methods/create). The full set of top-level `status` values will be confirmed by the smoke test; the client accepts only `completed` either way.\n\n## Team decisions (from review)\n\n- **Uncited answers:** keep rejecting `answered` replies with no citations. ✅ Done.\n- **Decline citations:** strict rejection. ✅ Done.\n- **Model:** no default in code. Neel suggested `gpt-6-luna` for the first controlled evaluation, if our API project supports it. It is set through `OPENAI_QA_MODEL`, not in code.\n- **Live test:** one controlled smoke test with synthetic data after the integration contract and spending limit are approved, verified by Shawn or Deep, then the S2-004 evaluation suite before enabling Q&A. `QA_ENABLED` stays `false` until then.\n\n## Remaining gates before marking ready\n\n- [ ] #76 merged, then this PR retargeted to `main`\n- [ ] Redacted S2-011 AI transcript committed (Section 6)\n- [ ] Independent §7 security review of the OpenAI data boundary\n- [ ] Not in this PR: Q&A route and UI (S2-009), S2-010 wiring after #78, live smoke test and S2-004 evaluation\n\n## Testing\n\nLocal, at `7a2aaf1`:\n- `npm run format:check` ✅\n- `npm run lint` ✅\n- `npm run typecheck` ✅\n- `npm test`: 52 suites, 774 tests passed ✅ (QA modules: 100% statements, branches, functions, lines)\n- `npm run build` ✅\n\nGitHub CI at `7a2aaf1`: **Lint, typecheck, test, build** ✅ and **Database tests** ✅ ([run](https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/actions/runs/43506068340)).\n\n## AI assistance\n\nClaude Code was used for design, implementation, tests, and docs. 1. **Citations on non-answers.** If an `insufficient_evidence` or decline reply cites real review IDs, it's now rejected (`UNEXPECTED_CITATIONS`) instead of the citations being silently dropped. Citing an unknown ID is rejected for every outcome. There are tests for all four non-answer outcomes, each with no citations, valid citations, and invalid citations.
   - I also changed the prompt. It used to tell the model to put partial answers under `insufficient_evidence` with citations, which the new rule would always reject. Partial answers now use `answered` with citations, and every other outcome must have empty citations. The prompt version is now `s2-013.v3`.
2. **Answers with no citations.** These are still rejected. I added a code comment and a "Known limitations" section to the PR: the check confirms cited IDs belong to the dataset, not that the review supports the claim. A test shows a parking claim citing a food review gets through. S2-004 covers that kind of check.
3. **Model setting.** The model comes only from `OPENAI_QA_MODEL`, with no fallback. A missing model and a missing key now get separate error codes. Each error also carries a log-only note, such as "OPENAI_QA_MODEL is not set. There is no default model…". Users still see a generic message.
4. **Request budget.** The limits are unchanged: a 16,000-token cap with 1,500 reserved for output. A new test sends an oversized request through the real OpenAI adapter and shows it never reaches the provider.
5. **Off by default.** New tests show neither the injected nor the global `fetch` is called when `QA_ENABLED` is `false` or unset. Another test checks that `.env.example` ships with Q&A off and no model set.
6. **PR description.** It now covers:
   - the entry point: the route calls `answerQuestion()`, which is built on `runGroundedQaRequest()`. Calling `runGroundedQaRequest()` directly would skip the reply check, so I named both.
